2026-10-11T18:47:57.8160872Z  	CREATE FUNCTION "journal_migration_8afdeacc5ca74f17980e1b3c6636ff62".capture_workflow_event_journal() RETURNS trigger
2026-10-11T18:47:57.8160983Z  	LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY INVOKER SET search_path=pg_catalog AS $$
2026-10-11T18:47:57.8161032Z  	DECLARE
2026-10-11T18:47:57.8161123Z  	 c jsonb; r jsonb; ledger_state text; i record; t record;

2026-10-11T18:47:57.8161294Z  	 wanted_source text;
2026-10-11T18:47:57.8161342Z  	BEGIN
2026-10-11T18:47:57.8161452Z  	 SELECT command_json,receipt_json,state INTO STRICT c,r,ledger_state

2026-10-11T18:47:57.8161849Z  	 SELECT wi.*, wv.version_id AS original_version_id, wd.name AS original_name INTO STRICT i
2026-10-11T18:47:57.8161975Z  	 FROM "journal_migration_8afdeacc5ca74f17980e1b3c6636ff62".workflow_instances wi
2026-10-11T18:47:57.8162278Z  	 JOIN "journal_migration_8afdeacc5ca74f17980e1b3c6636ff62".workflow_definitions wd ON wd.app_id=wi.app_id AND wd.id=wi.flow_id
2026-10-11T18:47:57.8162545Z  	 JOIN "journal_migration_8afdeacc5ca74f17980e1b3c6636ff62".workflow_versions wv ON wv.app_id=wi.app_id AND wv.flow_id=wi.flow_id AND wv.version=wi.definition_version
2026-10-11T18:47:57.8162634Z  	 WHERE wi.app_id=NEW.app_id AND wi.id=NEW.instance_id;
2026-10-11T18:47:57.8162723Z  	 IF (c->>'ProtocolVersion')::integer IS DISTINCT FROM 2
2026-10-11T18:47:57.8162813Z  	 OR (c->>'CommandID')::uuid IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.command_id
2026-10-11T18:47:57.8162973Z  	 OR (c->>'AppID')::uuid IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.app_id
2026-10-11T18:47:57.8163064Z  	 OR (c->>'InstanceID')::uuid IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.instance_id

2026-10-11T18:47:57.8163214Z  	 OR c->>'Action' IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.action
2026-10-11T18:47:57.8163315Z  	 OR (c->>'SchemaVersion')::bigint IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.schema_version
2026-10-11T18:47:57.8163416Z  	 OR (c->>'RecordVersion')::bigint IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.record_version
